private lemma a52_density_step (N n n' c c' : ℝ) (K : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) (hn' : 0 < n') (hN : 0 ≤ N)
    (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hc' : c' ≤ 3 * c) (hr : n ^ 2 ≤ 4 * N * n')
    (h : c * (16 * N / n) ^ (2 ^ (K + 2)) ≤ N) :
    c' * (16 * N / n') ^ (2 ^ (K + 1)) ≤ N := by
  set x := 16 * N / n with hx
  have hx0 : 0 ≤ x := by positivity
  have h1 : 16 * N / n' ≤ x ^ 2 / 4 := by
    rw [hx, div_pow, div_div, div_le_div_iff₀ hn' (by positivity)]
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hr (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 16 * N)]
  set m := 2 ^ (K + 1) with hm
  have hm1 : 1 ≤ m := Nat.one_le_two_pow
  have h2 : (16 * N / n') ^ m ≤ x ^ (2 ^ (K + 2)) / 4 := by
    calc (16 * N / n') ^ m ≤ (x ^ 2 / 4) ^ m := pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) h1 m
      _ = x ^ (2 ^ (K + 2)) / 4 ^ m := by
          rw [div_pow, ← pow_mul]; congr 2; rw [hm, pow_succ 2 (K + 1)]; ring
      _ ≤ x ^ (2 ^ (K + 2)) / 4 := by
          apply div_le_div_of_nonneg_left (by positivity) (by norm_num)
          calc (4:ℝ) = 4 ^ 1 := by norm_num
            _ ≤ 4 ^ m := pow_le_pow_right₀ (by norm_num) hm1
  calc c' * (16 * N / n') ^ m ≤ (3 * c) * (x ^ (2 ^ (K + 2)) / 4) :=
        mul_le_mul hc' h2 (by positivity) (by positivity)
    _ ≤ c * x ^ (2 ^ (K + 2)) := by nlinarith [pow_nonneg hx0 (2 ^ (K + 2))]
    _ ≤ N := h

-- The density hypothesis forces `A` to be large compared with the forbidden set.
private lemma a52_size_bound (N n c : ℝ) (M : ℕ) (hM : 2 ≤ M) (hn : 0 < n) (hnN : n ≤ N)
    (hc : 0 ≤ c) (h : c * (16 * N / n) ^ M ≤ N) : 256 * c ≤ n := by
  set x := 16 * N / n with hx
  have hN : 0 < N := lt_of_lt_of_le hn hnN
  have hx16 : 16 ≤ x := by rw [hx, le_div_iff₀ hn]; linarith
  have h1 : x ^ 2 ≤ x ^ M := pow_le_pow_right₀ (by linarith) hM
  have h2 : c * (16 * x) ≤ N := by nlinarith
  have h3 : 256 * c * N ≤ n * N := by
    have : c * (16 * x) * n = 256 * c * N := by rw [hx]; field_simp; ring
    nlinarith
  exact le_of_mul_le_mul_right h3 hN

-- Cube lemma with finitely many nontrivial linear constraints `α * a + ∑ c_k v_k + f ≠ 0`
-- on the base point `a` and the generators `v`.
private lemma a52_cube_lin_aux (K : ℕ) : ∀ (N : ℕ) (A : Finset ℕ)
    (C : Finset ((Fin K → ℤ) × ℤ × ℤ)), A ⊆ Finset.Icc 1 N → A.Nonempty →
    (∀ q ∈ C, q.2.1 ≠ 0 ∨ q.1 ≠ 0) →
    ((C.card : ℝ) + 1) * (16 * (N : ℝ) / A.card) ^ (2 ^ (K + 1)) ≤ N →
    ∃ (a : ℕ) (v : Fin K → ℕ), (∀ k, 0 < v k) ∧
      (∀ q ∈ C, q.2.1 * (a : ℤ) + ∑ k, q.1 k * (v k : ℤ) + q.2.2 ≠ 0) ∧
      ∀ ε, a + a52_cubeSum v ε ∈ A := by
  induction K with
  | zero =>
    intro N A C hA hne hC h
    have hn0 : 0 < A.card := hne.card_pos
    have hnN : A.card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_card hA
    have hbig : 256 * ((C.card : ℝ) + 1) ≤ A.card :=
      a52_size_bound N A.card _ (2 ^ (0 + 1)) (by norm_num)
        (by exact_mod_cast hn0) (by exact_mod_cast hnN) (by positivity) h
    have hlt : (C.image (fun q => (-q.2.2 / q.2.1).toNat)).card < A.card := by
      have h1 : (C.image (fun q => (-q.2.2 / q.2.1).toNat)).card ≤ C.card :=
        Finset.card_image_le
      have : (C.card : ℝ) < A.card := by linarith
      have : C.card < A.card := by exact_mod_cast this
      omega
    obtain ⟨a, haA, haB⟩ := Finset.exists_mem_notMem_of_card_lt_card hlt
    refine ⟨a, Fin.elim0, fun k => k.elim0, ?_, fun ε => by simpa [a52_cubeSum] using haA⟩
    intro q hq heq
    have hα : q.2.1 ≠ 0 := by
      rcases hC q hq with h | h
      · exact h
      · exact absurd (funext fun k => k.elim0) h
    simp only [Finset.univ_eq_empty, Finset.sum_empty, add_zero] at heq
    apply haB
    refine Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨q, hq, ?_⟩
    have : -q.2.2 / q.2.1 = a := by
      rw [show -q.2.2 = q.2.1 * a by linarith]; exact Int.mul_ediv_cancel_left _ hα
    rw [this]; simp
  | succ K ih =>
    intro N A C hA hne hC h
    have hn0 : 0 < A.card := hne.card_pos
    have hnN : A.card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_card hA
    have hbig : 256 * ((C.card : ℝ) + 1) ≤ A.card :=
      a52_size_bound N A.card _ (2 ^ (K + 1 + 1))
        (by calc 2 = 2 ^ 1 := by norm_num
              _ ≤ 2 ^ (K + 1 + 1) := Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by norm_num) (by omega))
        (by exact_mod_cast hn0) (by exact_mod_cast hnN) (by positivity) h
    set F : Finset ℤ := C.image (fun q => -q.2.2 / q.1 0) with hF_def
    have hFc : F.card ≤ C.card := Finset.card_image_le
    have hbig' : 16 * (F.card + 1) ≤ A.card := by
      have : (16 : ℝ) * (C.card + 1) ≤ A.card := by linarith
      have : 16 * (C.card + 1) ≤ A.card := by exact_mod_cast this
      omega
    obtain ⟨d, hdI, hdr, hdF, -⟩ := a52_good_d_exists N A F hA hbig'
    have hd0 : 0 < d := (Finset.mem_Icc.1 hdI).1
    set A' := A.filter (fun x => x + d ∈ A) with hA'_def
    set C' : Finset ((Fin K → ℤ) × ℤ × ℤ) :=
      (C.filter (fun q => q.2.1 ≠ 0 ∨ Fin.tail q.1 ≠ 0)).image
        (fun q => (Fin.tail q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2 + q.1 0 * d)) with hC'_def
    have hC'card : C'.card ≤ C.card :=
      Finset.card_image_le.trans (Finset.card_filter_le _ _)
    have hA'sub : A' ⊆ Finset.Icc 1 N := (Finset.filter_subset _ _).trans hA
    have hA'pos : 0 < A'.card := by
      rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos A'.card with hc | hc
      · rw [hc, mul_zero, Nat.le_zero, pow_eq_zero_iff two_ne_zero] at hdr
        omega
      · exact hc
    have hC' : ∀ q ∈ C', q.2.1 ≠ 0 ∨ q.1 ≠ 0 := by
      intro q hq
      obtain ⟨q0, hq0, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hq
      exact (Finset.mem_filter.1 hq0).2
    have hstep : ((C'.card : ℝ) + 1) * (16 * (N : ℝ) / A'.card) ^ (2 ^ (K + 1)) ≤ N := by
      have hcc : (C'.card : ℝ) ≤ C.card := by exact_mod_cast hC'card
      refine a52_density_step N A.card A'.card (C.card + 1) (C'.card + 1) K
        (by exact_mod_cast hn0) (by exact_mod_cast hA'pos) (by positivity) (by positivity)
        (by linarith) (by exact_mod_cast hdr) h
    obtain ⟨a, v, hv, hlin, hmem⟩ := ih N A' C' hA'sub (Finset.card_pos.1 hA'pos) hC' hstep
    refine ⟨a, Fin.cons d v, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro k
      refine Fin.cases hd0 (fun i => ?_) k
      simpa using hv i
    · intro q hq
      rw [Fin.sum_univ_succ]
      simp only [Fin.cons_zero, Fin.cons_succ]
      by_cases hgood : q.2.1 ≠ 0 ∨ Fin.tail q.1 ≠ 0
      · have := hlin _ (Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨q, Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨hq, hgood⟩, rfl⟩)
        simp only [Fin.tail] at this
        intro h; apply this; linarith
      · push_neg at hgood
        obtain ⟨hα, htail⟩ := hgood
        have hts : ∀ i : Fin K, q.1 i.succ = 0 := fun i => congrFun htail i
        have hc0 : q.1 0 ≠ 0 := by
          rcases hC q hq with h | h
          · exact absurd hα h
          · intro h0; apply h; funext k
            exact Fin.cases h0 (fun i => hts i) k
        simp only [hα, hts, zero_mul, Finset.sum_const_zero, add_zero, zero_add]
        intro heq
        apply hdF
        refine Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨q, hq, ?_⟩
        rw [show -q.2.2 = q.1 0 * d by linarith]; exact Int.mul_ediv_cancel_left _ hc0
    · intro ε
      rw [a52_cubeSum_cons]
      have hm := hmem (Fin.tail ε)
      rw [hA'_def, Finset.mem_filter] at hm
      cases ε 0
      · simpa using hm.1
      · simpa [add_comm, add_left_comm, add_assoc] using hm.2

-- Constraints encoding distinctness of the cube vertices.
private def a52_vertexConstraints (K : ℕ) : Finset ((Fin K → ℤ) × ℤ × ℤ) :=
  ((Finset.univ : Finset ((Fin K → Bool) × (Fin K → Bool))).filter (fun p => p.1 ≠ p.2)).image
    (fun p => (fun k => (if p.1 k then 1 else 0) - (if p.2 k then 1 else 0), 0, 0))

-- Coefficient of `v k` in the shift `r(ε, h)`.
private def a52_shiftCoeff {K : ℕ} (ε : Fin K → Bool) (h : ℕ) (k : Fin K) : ℤ :=
  if ε k then ((h + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((k.1 : ℤ) + 1) else 0

-- Constraints encoding distinctness of the shifts.
private def a52_shiftConstraints (K H : ℕ) : Finset ((Fin K → ℤ) × ℤ × ℤ) :=
  ((Finset.univ : Finset (((Fin K → Bool) × Fin H) × ((Fin K → Bool) × Fin H))).filter
    (fun p => p.1 ≠ p.2)).image
    (fun p => (fun k => a52_shiftCoeff p.1.1 p.1.2.1 k - a52_shiftCoeff p.2.1 p.2.2.1 k,
      ((p.1.2.1 + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((p.2.2.1 + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ), 0))

private lemma a52_constraints_nontrivial (K H : ℕ) :
    ∀ q ∈ a52_vertexConstraints K ∪ a52_shiftConstraints K H, q.2.1 ≠ 0 ∨ q.1 ≠ 0 := by
  intro q hq
  rcases Finset.mem_union.1 hq with hq | hq
  · obtain ⟨⟨ε, ε'⟩, hp, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hq
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and] at hp
    right
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Function.ne_iff.1 hp
    intro h0
    have := congrFun h0 k
    simp only [Pi.zero_apply] at this
    cases h1 : ε k <;> cases h2 : ε' k <;> simp_all
  · obtain ⟨⟨⟨ε, h⟩, ⟨ε', h'⟩⟩, hp, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hq
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and] at hp
    by_cases hh : h = h'
    · subst hh
      right
      have hne : ε ≠ ε' := fun he => hp (by rw [he])
      obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Function.ne_iff.1 hne
      intro h0
      have := congrFun h0 k
      simp only [Pi.zero_apply, a52_shiftCoeff] at this
      have hk2 := k.2
      cases h1 : ε k <;> cases h2 : ε' k <;> simp_all <;> omega
    · left
      intro h0
      apply hh
      apply Fin.ext
      push_cast at h0
      omega

private lemma a52_vertex_diff {K : ℕ} (a : ℤ) (v : Fin K → ℤ) (ε ε' : Fin K → Bool) :
    (a + ∑ k, if ε k then v k else 0) - (a + ∑ k, if ε' k then v k else 0) =
      ∑ k, ((if ε k then 1 else 0) - (if ε' k then 1 else 0)) * v k := by
  rw [add_sub_add_left_eq_sub, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
  split_ifs <;> ring

private lemma a52_shift_diff {K : ℕ} (a : ℤ) (v : Fin K → ℤ) (ε ε' : Fin K → Bool) (h h' : ℕ) :
    ((a + ∑ k, if ε k then v k else 0) * ((h + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ)
          - ∑ k : Fin K, ((k.1 : ℤ) + 1) * (if ε k then v k else 0)) -
      ((a + ∑ k, if ε' k then v k else 0) * ((h' + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ)
          - ∑ k : Fin K, ((k.1 : ℤ) + 1) * (if ε' k then v k else 0)) =
      (((h + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((h' + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ)) * a +
        ∑ k, (a52_shiftCoeff ε h k - a52_shiftCoeff ε' h' k) * v k := by
  have : ∀ k, (a52_shiftCoeff ε h k - a52_shiftCoeff ε' h' k) * v k =
      (if ε k then v k else 0) * ((h + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((k.1 : ℤ) + 1) * (if ε k then v k else 0)
        - ((if ε' k then v k else 0) * ((h' + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ)
          - ((k.1 : ℤ) + 1) * (if ε' k then v k else 0)) := by
    intro k; simp only [a52_shiftCoeff]; split_ifs <;> ring
  simp only [this, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, add_mul, Finset.sum_mul]
  ring

/-- Qualitative Lemma 5.2 of TT (arXiv:2512.01739v2, p. 50), without the window `[P/2, P]`:
for all `K, H` there are `p₀, v` with the `2^K` numbers `p_ε = p₀ + ∑ ε_k v_k` distinct primes and
the shifts `r_{ε,h+K} = p_ε (h+K) - ∑_k k ε_k v_k` (`ε ∈ {0,1}^K`, `1 ≤ h ≤ H`) pairwise distinct.
(The paper needs the quantitative form with the primes in `[P/2, P]`.) -/

-- Asymptotic inequality behind the quantitative cube lemma (θ < 1).
private lemma a52_quant_asymp_real (θ D : ℝ) (hθ : θ < 1) :
    ∀ᶠ y : ℝ in Filter.atTop, 4 * Real.log y + 2 * y ^ θ * (Real.log D + Real.log y) + Real.log 6 ≤ y := by
  have h1 : (fun y : ℝ => Real.log y) =o[Filter.atTop] (fun y => y) := Real.isLittleO_log_id_atTop
  have hr : 0 < 1 - θ := by linarith
  have h3 : (fun y : ℝ => y ^ θ * (Real.log D + Real.log y)) =o[Filter.atTop] (fun y => y) := by
    have hc : (fun _ : ℝ => Real.log D) =o[Filter.atTop] (fun y : ℝ => y ^ (1 - θ)) :=
      Asymptotics.isLittleO_const_left.2
        (Or.inr (tendsto_norm_atTop_atTop.comp (tendsto_rpow_atTop hr)))
    have hl : (fun y : ℝ => Real.log D + Real.log y) =o[Filter.atTop] (fun y => y ^ (1 - θ)) :=
      hc.add (isLittleO_log_rpow_atTop hr)
    have := (Asymptotics.isBigO_refl (fun y : ℝ => y ^ θ) Filter.atTop).mul_isLittleO hl
    refine this.congr' Filter.EventuallyEq.rfl ?_
    filter_upwards [Filter.eventually_gt_atTop 0] with y hy
    rw [← Real.rpow_add hy]; simp
  have h4 : (fun _ : ℝ => (1:ℝ)) =o[Filter.atTop] (fun y => y) := Asymptotics.isLittleO_const_id_atTop 1
  have hsum : (fun y : ℝ => 4 * Real.log y + 2 * y ^ θ * (Real.log D + Real.log y) + Real.log 6)
      =o[Filter.atTop] (fun y => y) := by
    have := ((h1.const_mul_left 4).add (h3.const_mul_left 2)).add (h4.const_mul_left (Real.log 6))
    refine this.congr_left (fun y => by ring)
  filter_upwards [hsum.bound one_pos, Filter.eventually_ge_atTop 0] with y hy hy0
  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg hy0, one_mul] at hy
  exact (le_abs_self _).trans hy

-- The same inequality along naturals `P`, in the shape needed for `a52_cube_lin_aux`.
private lemma a52_quant_asymp_nat (θ D : ℝ) (hθ : θ < 1) (hD : 1 ≤ D) :
    ∃ P1 : ℕ, ∀ P : ℕ, P1 ≤ P → 1 ≤ Real.log P ∧
      3 * Real.log P ^ 4 * (D * Real.log P) ^ (2 * Real.log P ^ θ) ≤ (P : ℝ) / 2 := by
  have ht : Filter.Tendsto (fun P : ℕ => Real.log P) Filter.atTop Filter.atTop :=
    Real.tendsto_log_atTop.comp tendsto_natCast_atTop_atTop
  obtain ⟨P1, hP1⟩ := Filter.eventually_atTop.1
    (ht.eventually ((a52_quant_asymp_real θ D hθ).and (Filter.eventually_ge_atTop 1)))
  refine ⟨P1, fun P hP => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨h, hy1⟩ := hP1 P hP
  refine ⟨hy1, ?_⟩
  set y := Real.log P with hy
  have hP0 : (0 : ℝ) < P := by
    by_contra hc
    have : (P : ℝ) = 0 := le_antisymm (not_lt.1 hc) (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
    rw [hy, this, Real.log_zero] at hy1; linarith
  have hy0 : 0 < y := by linarith
  have hDy : 0 < D * y := by positivity
  have hD0 : 0 < D := by linarith
  rw [← Real.log_le_log_iff (by positivity) (by positivity), Real.log_mul (by positivity)
    (by positivity), Real.log_mul (by positivity) (by positivity), Real.log_pow,
    Real.log_rpow hDy, Real.log_mul hD0.ne' hy0.ne', Real.log_div hP0.ne' (by norm_num),
    ← hy]
  have h6 : Real.log 6 = Real.log 3 + Real.log 2 := by
    rw [← Real.log_mul (by norm_num) (by norm_num)]; norm_num
  push_cast
  linarith

-- Counting bound on the constraint set.
private lemma a52_quant_constraints_card (K H : ℕ) :
    ((a52_vertexConstraints K ∪ a52_shiftConstraints K H).card : ℝ) ≤
      ((2 : ℝ) ^ K) ^ 2 + ((2 : ℝ) ^ K * H) ^ 2 := by
  have h1 : (a52_vertexConstraints K).card ≤ (2 ^ K) ^ 2 := by
    unfold a52_vertexConstraints
    refine (Finset.card_image_le).trans ((Finset.card_filter_le _ _).trans ?_)
    rw [Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_prod, Fintype.card_fun, Fintype.card_bool, Fintype.card_fin,
      sq]
  have h2 : (a52_shiftConstraints K H).card ≤ (2 ^ K * H) ^ 2 := by
    unfold a52_shiftConstraints
    refine (Finset.card_image_le).trans ((Finset.card_filter_le _ _).trans ?_)
    rw [Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_prod, Fintype.card_prod, Fintype.card_fun, Fintype.card_bool,
      Fintype.card_fin, Fintype.card_fin, sq]
  have h3 := (Finset.card_union_le (a52_vertexConstraints K) (a52_shiftConstraints K H)).trans
    (Nat.add_le_add h1 h2)
  exact_mod_cast h3


/- Quantitative Lemma 5.2 of Tao–Teräväinen (arXiv:2512.01739v2, p. 50): K and H may grow with P.
For every θ < 1 and all large P: whenever 2^K ≤ (log P)^θ and H ≤ (log P)^θ, there are p₀, v with
the 2^K numbers p_ε = p₀ + Σ ε_k v_k distinct primes in the window (P/2, P], and the shifts
r_{ε,h+K} = p_ε (h+K) − Σ_k k ε_k v_k (ε ∈ {0,1}^K, 1 ≤ h ≤ H) pairwise distinct.
(The paper takes 2^K ≈ H ≈ (log P)^{2/3}; see `a52_hilbert_cube_primes_quant_two_thirds`.) -/

private theorem a52_hilbert_cube_primes_quant (θ : ℝ) (hθ : θ < 1) :
    ∃ P0 : ℕ, ∀ P : ℕ, P0 ≤ P → ∀ K H : ℕ,
      (2 : ℝ) ^ K ≤ Real.log P ^ θ → (H : ℝ) ≤ Real.log P ^ θ →
      ∃ (p0 : ℤ) (v : Fin K → ℤ),
        (∀ ε : Fin K → Bool, ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ P < 2 * p ∧ p ≤ P ∧
            (p : ℤ) = p0 + ∑ k, if ε k then v k else 0) ∧
        Function.Injective (fun ε : Fin K → Bool => p0 + ∑ k, if ε k then v k else 0) ∧
        Function.Injective (fun x : (Fin K → Bool) × Fin H =>
          (p0 + ∑ k, if x.1 k then v k else 0) * ((x.2.1 + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ)
            - ∑ k : Fin K, ((k.1 : ℤ) + 1) * (if x.1 k then v k else 0)) := by
  obtain ⟨c, hc, N0, hN0⟩ := a52_primes_in_dyadic_lower
  set D : ℝ := 32 / c + 1 with hD
  have hD1 : 1 ≤ D := by rw [hD]; linarith [div_pos (by norm_num : (0:ℝ) < 32) hc]
  obtain ⟨P1, hP1⟩ := a52_quant_asymp_nat θ D hθ hD1
  refine ⟨max P1 (2 * N0 + 6), fun P hP K H hK hH => ?_⟩
  have hPP1 : P1 ≤ P := le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hP
  have hPN : 2 * N0 + 6 ≤ P := le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hP
  obtain ⟨hy1, hasym⟩ := hP1 P hPP1
  set C := a52_vertexConstraints K ∪ a52_shiftConstraints K H with hCdef
  set n := P / 2 with hndef
  have hnN0 : N0 ≤ n := by omega
  have hn3 : 3 ≤ n := by omega
  set A := (Finset.Ioc n (2 * n)).filter Nat.Prime with hAdef
  set y := Real.log P with hy
  set L := y ^ θ with hL
  have hnr : (3 : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn3
  have hlogn : 0 < Real.log n := Real.log_pos (by linarith)
  have hlognP : Real.log n ≤ y :=
    Real.log_le_log (by linarith) (by exact_mod_cast Nat.div_le_self P 2)
  have hcard : c * (n : ℝ) / Real.log n ≤ (A.card : ℝ) := hN0 n hnN0
  have hApos : (0 : ℝ) < A.card := lt_of_lt_of_le (by positivity) hcard
  have hne : A.Nonempty := Finset.card_pos.mp (by exact_mod_cast hApos)
  have hsub : A ⊆ Finset.Icc 1 (2 * n) := by
    intro x hx
    simp only [hAdef, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_Ioc] at hx
    exact Finset.mem_Icc.mpr ⟨by omega, hx.1.2⟩
  have hbound : 16 * ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) / A.card ≤ D * y := by
    rw [div_le_iff₀ hApos]
    calc 16 * ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) = (32 / c * Real.log n) * (c * n / Real.log n) := by
          push_cast; field_simp; ring
      _ ≤ (32 / c * Real.log n) * A.card :=
          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hcard (by positivity)
      _ ≤ (D * y) * A.card := by
          apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hApos.le
          have h32 : 0 ≤ 32 / c := by positivity
          calc 32 / c * Real.log n ≤ 32 / c * y := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hlognP h32
            _ ≤ D * y := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by rw [hD]; linarith) (by linarith)
  have hL1 : 1 ≤ L := le_trans (one_le_pow₀ (by norm_num)) hK
  have hLy : L ≤ y := by
    have := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hy1 hθ.le
    rwa [Real.rpow_one] at this
  have hH0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ H := Nat.cast_nonneg H
  have h2K0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2 ^ K := by positivity
  have hCcard : (C.card : ℝ) + 1 ≤ 3 * y ^ 4 := by
    have h1 := a52_quant_constraints_card K H
    have hKH : (2 : ℝ) ^ K * H ≤ L * L := mul_le_mul hK hH hH0 (by linarith)
    have hsq1 : ((2 : ℝ) ^ K) ^ 2 ≤ L ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ h2K0 hK 2
    have hsq2 : ((2 : ℝ) ^ K * H) ^ 2 ≤ (L * L) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) hKH 2
    have hL4 : L ^ 2 ≤ L ^ 4 := pow_le_pow_right₀ hL1 (by norm_num)
    have hL4' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ L ^ 4 := one_le_pow₀ hL1
    have hLy4 : L ^ 4 ≤ y ^ 4 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (by linarith) hLy 4
    have hC : (C.card : ℝ) ≤ ((2 : ℝ) ^ K) ^ 2 + ((2 : ℝ) ^ K * H) ^ 2 := h1
    nlinarith
  have hDy1 : 1 ≤ D * y := by nlinarith
  have hX0 : 0 ≤ 16 * ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) / A.card := by positivity
  have hexp : (((2 ^ (K + 1) : ℕ) : ℝ)) ≤ 2 * L := by
    push_cast; rw [pow_succ]; linarith
  have hXpow : (16 * ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) / A.card) ^ (2 ^ (K + 1)) ≤ (D * y) ^ (2 * L) := by
    calc (16 * ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) / A.card) ^ (2 ^ (K + 1)) ≤ (D * y) ^ (2 ^ (K + 1)) :=
          pow_le_pow_left₀ hX0 hbound _
      _ = (D * y) ^ (((2 ^ (K + 1) : ℕ) : ℝ)) := (Real.rpow_natCast _ _).symm
      _ ≤ (D * y) ^ (2 * L) := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hDy1 hexp
  have hNP : (P : ℝ) / 2 ≤ ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
    have h1 : P ≤ 2 * n + 1 := by omega
    have h2 : (P : ℝ) ≤ ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) + 1 := by exact_mod_cast h1
    have h3 : (6 : ℝ) ≤ P := by exact_mod_cast (le_trans (by omega) hPN)
    linarith
  have hpow : ((C.card : ℝ) + 1) * (16 * ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) / A.card) ^ (2 ^ (K + 1)) ≤
      ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
    calc ((C.card : ℝ) + 1) * (16 * ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) / A.card) ^ (2 ^ (K + 1))
        ≤ (3 * y ^ 4) * (D * y) ^ (2 * L) :=
          mul_le_mul hCcard hXpow (by positivity) (by positivity)
      _ ≤ (P : ℝ) / 2 := hasym
      _ ≤ _ := hNP
  obtain ⟨a, v, -, hlin, hmem⟩ :=
    a52_cube_lin_aux K (2 * n) A C hsub hne (a52_constraints_nontrivial K H) hpow
  have hcast : ∀ ε : Fin K → Bool,
      ((a + a52_cubeSum v ε : ℕ) : ℤ) = (a : ℤ) + ∑ k, if ε k then (v k : ℤ) else 0 := by
    intro ε; simp [a52_cubeSum, Nat.cast_sum, Nat.cast_ite]
  refine ⟨a, fun k => v k, fun ε => ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · have h := hmem ε
    simp only [hAdef, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_Ioc] at h
    exact ⟨_, h.2, by omega, by omega, hcast ε⟩
  · intro ε ε' heq
    by_contra hne'
    have hq : ((fun k => (if ε k then (1 : ℤ) else 0) - (if ε' k then 1 else 0)), (0 : ℤ),
        (0 : ℤ)) ∈ C :=
      Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨(ε, ε'), Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, hne'⟩, rfl⟩)
    apply hlin _ hq
    have hd := a52_vertex_diff (a : ℤ) (fun k => (v k : ℤ)) ε ε'
    simp only at heq hd ⊢
    rw [heq, sub_self] at hd
    simp only [zero_mul, zero_add, add_zero]
    exact hd.symm
  · rintro ⟨ε, h⟩ ⟨ε', h'⟩ heq
    by_contra hne'
    have hq : ((fun k => a52_shiftCoeff ε h.1 k - a52_shiftCoeff ε' h'.1 k),
        ((h.1 + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((h'.1 + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ), (0 : ℤ)) ∈ C :=
      Finset.mem_union_right _
        (Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨((ε, h), (ε', h')), Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, hne'⟩, rfl⟩)
    apply hlin _ hq
    have hd := a52_shift_diff (a : ℤ) (fun k => (v k : ℤ)) ε ε' h.1 h'.1
    simp only at heq hd ⊢
    rw [heq, sub_self] at hd
    rw [add_zero]
    exact hd.symm

private theorem a52_hilbert_cube_primes_quant_two_thirds :
    ∃ P0 : ℕ, ∀ P : ℕ, P0 ≤ P → ∀ K H : ℕ,
      (2 : ℝ) ^ K ≤ Real.log P ^ ((2 : ℝ) / 3) → (H : ℝ) ≤ Real.log P ^ ((2 : ℝ) / 3) →
      ∃ (p0 : ℤ) (v : Fin K → ℤ),
        (∀ ε : Fin K → Bool, ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ P < 2 * p ∧ p ≤ P ∧
            (p : ℤ) = p0 + ∑ k, if ε k then v k else 0) ∧
        Function.Injective (fun ε : Fin K → Bool => p0 + ∑ k, if ε k then v k else 0) ∧
        Function.Injective (fun x : (Fin K → Bool) × Fin H =>
          (p0 + ∑ k, if x.1 k then v k else 0) * ((x.2.1 + 1 + K : ℕ) : ℤ)
            - ∑ k : Fin K, ((k.1 : ℤ) + 1) * (if x.1 k then v k else 0)) :=
  a52_hilbert_cube_primes_quant (2 / 3) (by norm_num)

private theorem mf_log_factorial (n : ℕ) :
    Real.log (n.factorial : ℝ) =
      ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, (padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) * Real.log p := by
  have hsub : (n.factorial).factorization.support ⊆ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime := by
    intro p hp
    rw [Nat.support_factorization] at hp
    have hpp := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hp
    have hdvd := Nat.dvd_of_mem_primeFactors hp
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_Iic]
    exact ⟨(hpp.dvd_factorial).1 hdvd, hpp⟩
  rw [Real.log_nat_eq_sum_factorization, Finsupp.sum_of_support_subset _ hsub _ (by intros; simp)]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro p hp
  rw [Nat.factorization_def _ (Finset.mem_filter.1 hp).2]

/-- `v_p(n!) ≥ ⌊n/p⌋ > n/p - 1`. -/
private theorem mf_padic_lower (n p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    (n : ℝ) / p - 1 ≤ (padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) := by
  haveI := Fact.mk hp
  have hp0 : (0:ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.pos
  have h1 : n / p ≤ padicValNat p n.factorial := by
    rw [padicValNat_factorial (b := Nat.log p n + 1) (Nat.lt_succ_self _)]
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge n p with h | h
    · rw [Nat.div_eq_of_lt h]; exact Nat.zero_le _
    · have hlog : 0 < Nat.log p n := Nat.log_pos hp.one_lt h
      have hmem : 1 ∈ Finset.Ico 1 (Nat.log p n + 1) := by
        simp only [Finset.mem_Ico]; omega
      calc n / p = n / p ^ 1 := by rw [pow_one]
        _ ≤ _ := Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => n / p ^ i) (fun i _ => Nat.zero_le _) hmem
  have h2 : (n : ℝ) < ((n / p : ℕ) : ℝ) * p + p := by
    exact_mod_cast Nat.lt_div_mul_add hp.pos
  have h3 : (n : ℝ) / p - 1 < ((n / p : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
    rw [div_sub_one hp0.ne', div_lt_iff₀ hp0]; linarith
  have h4 : ((n / p : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ (padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h1
  linarith

/-- `(p - 1) v_p(n!) ≤ n`. -/
private theorem mf_padic_upper (n p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    ((p : ℝ) - 1) * (padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) ≤ n := by
  haveI := Fact.mk hp
  rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with rfl | hn
  · simp
  have h := sub_one_mul_padicValNat_factorial_lt_of_ne_zero p hn.ne'
  have h' : ((p - 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast h.le
  rwa [Nat.cast_sub hp.one_le, Nat.cast_one] at h'

/-- Chebyshev: `θ(n) ≤ n log 4`. -/
private theorem mf_theta (n : ℕ) :
    ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p ≤ Real.log 4 * n := by
  have h := Chebyshev.theta_le_log4_mul_x (x := (n : ℝ)) (Nat.cast_nonneg n)
  rw [Chebyshev.theta_eq_sum_Icc, Nat.floor_natCast] at h
  have : Finset.Icc 0 n = Finset.Iic n := by ext; simp
  rwa [this] at h

private theorem mf_log_fact_le (n : ℕ) : Real.log (n.factorial : ℝ) ≤ n * Real.log n := by
  have h0 : (0:ℝ) < n.factorial := by exact_mod_cast Nat.factorial_pos n
  have h : (n.factorial : ℝ) ≤ (n:ℝ) ^ n := by exact_mod_cast Nat.factorial_le_pow n
  calc Real.log (n.factorial : ℝ) ≤ Real.log ((n:ℝ)^n) := Real.log_le_log h0 h
    _ = n * Real.log n := by rw [Real.log_pow]

private theorem mf_log_fact_ge (n : ℕ) : n * Real.log n - n ≤ Real.log (n.factorial : ℝ) := by
  have h0 : (0:ℝ) < n.factorial := by exact_mod_cast Nat.factorial_pos n
  rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with rfl | hn
  · simp
  have h := Real.pow_div_factorial_le_exp (n : ℝ) (Nat.cast_nonneg n) n
  have hn' : (0:ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
  have hpos : (0:ℝ) < (n:ℝ)^n / n.factorial := by positivity
  have := Real.log_le_log hpos h
  rw [Real.log_exp, Real.log_div (by positivity) h0.ne', Real.log_pow] at this
  linarith

private theorem mf_term_le (p : ℕ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) :
    Real.log p / (p * (p - 1)) ≤ 4 * (1 / (p : ℝ) ^ (3/2 : ℝ)) := by
  have hp' : (2:ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp
  have hp0 : (0:ℝ) < p := by linarith
  have hs0 : 0 < (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hp0 _
  have hss : (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) * (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) = p := by
    rw [← Real.rpow_add hp0]; norm_num
  have h32 : (p:ℝ) ^ (3/2 : ℝ) = p * (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) := by
    rw [show (3/2:ℝ) = 1 + 1/2 by norm_num, Real.rpow_add hp0, Real.rpow_one]
  have hlog : Real.log p ≤ 2 * (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) := by
    have := Real.log_le_rpow_div (x := (p:ℝ)) (ε := 1/2) hp0.le (by norm_num)
    have e : (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) / (1/2) = 2 * (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) := by ring
    linarith
  have hlog0 : 0 ≤ Real.log p := Real.log_nonneg (by linarith)
  rw [h32, mul_one_div, div_le_div_iff₀ (by nlinarith) (by positivity)]
  have h1 : Real.log p * (p:ℝ) ^ (1/2 : ℝ) ≤ 2 * p := by nlinarith
  nlinarith

/-- `∑_{p ≤ n} log p / (p (p - 1))` is bounded. -/
private theorem mf_tail_bound : ∃ C0 : ℝ, ∀ n : ℕ,
    ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / (p * (p - 1)) ≤ C0 := by
  have hsum : Summable (fun m : ℕ => 4 * (1 / (m : ℝ) ^ (3/2 : ℝ))) :=
    (Real.summable_one_div_nat_rpow.2 (by norm_num)).mul_left 4
  refine ⟨∑' m : ℕ, 4 * (1 / (m : ℝ) ^ (3/2 : ℝ)), fun n => ?_⟩
  calc _ ≤ ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, 4 * (1 / (p : ℝ) ^ (3/2 : ℝ)) :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum fun p hp => mf_term_le p (Finset.mem_filter.1 hp).2.two_le
    _ ≤ _ := hsum.sum_le_tsum _ (fun i _ => by positivity)

/-- Mertens' first theorem: `∑_{p ≤ n} log p / p = log n + O(1)`. -/
private theorem u_mertens_first :
    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ n : ℕ, 2 ≤ n →
      |(∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) - Real.log n| ≤ C := by
  obtain ⟨C0, hC0⟩ := mf_tail_bound
  refine ⟨Real.log 4 + 1 + |C0|, fun n hn => ?_⟩
  have hn0 : (0:ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast (show 0 < n by omega)
  have hL := mf_log_factorial n
  have hup : (n:ℝ) * (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p)
      ≤ Real.log (n.factorial : ℝ) + Real.log 4 * n := by
    have : (n:ℝ) * (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) ≤
        ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime,
          ((padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) * Real.log p + Real.log p) := by
      rw [Finset.mul_sum]
      apply Finset.sum_le_sum
      intro p hp
      have hpp := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hp).2
      have hlog0 : 0 ≤ Real.log p := Real.log_nonneg (by exact_mod_cast hpp.one_lt.le)
      have hv := mf_padic_lower n p hpp
      have e : (n:ℝ) * (Real.log p / p) = ((n:ℝ) / p) * Real.log p := by ring
      rw [e]; nlinarith
    rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, ← hL] at this
    linarith [mf_theta n]
  have hlo : Real.log (n.factorial : ℝ) ≤
      (n:ℝ) * (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) + n * C0 := by
    rw [hL]
    have : ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, (padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) * Real.log p ≤
        ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime,
          ((n:ℝ) * (Real.log p / p) + n * (Real.log p / (p * (p - 1)))) := by
      apply Finset.sum_le_sum
      intro p hp
      have hpp := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hp).2
      have hp2 : (2:ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hpp.two_le
      have hpne : (p:ℝ) ≠ 0 := by linarith
      have hp1 : (p:ℝ) - 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
      have hlog0 : 0 ≤ Real.log p := Real.log_nonneg (by linarith)
      have hv := mf_padic_upper n p hpp
      have key : (n:ℝ) * (Real.log p / p) + n * (Real.log p / (p * (p - 1)))
          = (n:ℝ) / (p - 1) * Real.log p := by
        field_simp; ring
      rw [key]
      have : (padicValNat p n.factorial : ℝ) ≤ n / (p - 1) := by
        rw [le_div_iff₀ (by linarith)]; linarith
      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right this hlog0
    rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.mul_sum] at this
    have := hC0 n
    nlinarith
  have h1 := mf_log_fact_le n
  have h2 := mf_log_fact_ge n
  have hSup : (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) ≤ Real.log n + Real.log 4 := by
    by_contra h; push_neg at h; nlinarith
  have hSlo : Real.log n - 1 - C0 ≤ (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) := by
    by_contra h; push_neg at h; nlinarith
  have hl4 : 0 ≤ Real.log 4 := Real.log_nonneg (by norm_num)
  rw [abs_le]; constructor
  · linarith [le_abs_self C0]
  · linarith [abs_nonneg C0]


private theorem ms_Iic_succ (g : ℕ → ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
    ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic (n+1)).filter Nat.Prime, g p =
      ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, g p + if (n+1).Prime then g (n+1) else 0 := by
  have h : ∀ m : ℕ, Finset.Iic m = Finset.range (m+1) := by
    intro m; ext x; simp [Nat.lt_succ_iff]
  rw [h, h, Finset.sum_filter, Finset.sum_filter, Finset.sum_range_succ]

private theorem ms_Iic_two : (Finset.Iic 2).filter Nat.Prime = {2} := by
  ext x
  simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_Iic, Finset.mem_singleton]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
    interval_cases x
    · exact absurd h2 Nat.not_prime_zero
    · exact absurd h2 Nat.not_prime_one
    · rfl
  · rintro rfl; exact ⟨le_rfl, Nat.prime_two⟩

/-- Discrete Abel summation of `1/p = (log p / p) / log p`. -/
private theorem ms_abel (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) :
    ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, (1:ℝ) / p =
      (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) / Real.log n +
      ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n, (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic k).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) *
          (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1)) := by
  induction n, hn using Nat.le_induction with
  | base =>
    rw [ms_Iic_two]
    have hl2 : Real.log 2 ≠ 0 := (Real.log_pos (by norm_num)).ne'
    simp only [Finset.sum_singleton, Finset.Ico_self, Finset.sum_empty, Nat.cast_ofNat, add_zero]
    field_simp
  | succ n hn ih =>
    have hB := ms_Iic_succ (fun p : ℕ => (1:ℝ) / p) n
    have hA := ms_Iic_succ (fun p : ℕ => Real.log p / p) n
    rw [Finset.sum_Ico_succ_top hn, hB, hA, ih]
    have hm : ((n:ℝ) + 1) ≠ 0 := by positivity
    have hL : Real.log ((n:ℝ) + 1) ≠ 0 := (Real.log_pos (by
      have : (2:ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
      linarith)).ne'
    push_cast
    split_ifs with hp
    · field_simp
      ring
    · ring

private theorem ms_tele (F : ℕ → ℝ) (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n, (F (k+1) - F k) = F n - F 2 := by
  induction n, hn using Nat.le_induction with
  | base => simp
  | succ n hn ih => rw [Finset.sum_Ico_succ_top hn, ih]; ring

/-- For `k ≥ 2`, with `u = log k`, `v = log (k+1)`, `d = 1/u - 1/v`:
`loglog(k+1) - loglog k - d ≤ u d ≤ loglog(k+1) - loglog k` and `0 ≤ d`. -/
private theorem ms_point (k : ℕ) (hk : 2 ≤ k) :
    Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k)
        - (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1))
      ≤ Real.log k * (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) ∧
    Real.log k * (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1))
      ≤ Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k) ∧
    0 ≤ 1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1) := by
  have hk' : (2:ℝ) ≤ k := by exact_mod_cast hk
  have hu : 0 < Real.log k := Real.log_pos (by linarith)
  have huv : Real.log k < Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1) := Real.log_lt_log (by linarith) (by linarith)
  have hv : 0 < Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1) := by linarith
  have hvu1 : Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1) - Real.log k ≤ 1 := by
    have h := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (x := ((k:ℝ)+1)/k) (by positivity)
    rw [Real.log_div (by positivity) (by positivity)] at h
    have e : ((k:ℝ)+1)/k - 1 = 1/k := by field_simp; ring
    have : 1/(k:ℝ) ≤ 1 := by rw [div_le_one (by positivity)]; linarith
    linarith
  set u := Real.log k
  set v := Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)
  have hll : Real.log v - Real.log u = Real.log (v/u) := (Real.log_div hv.ne' hu.ne').symm
  have hvu0 : 0 < v / u := by positivity
  have hup := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos hvu0
  have hlo := Real.one_sub_inv_le_log_of_pos hvu0
  have e1 : u * (1/u - 1/v) = 1 - (v/u)⁻¹ := by field_simp
  have e2 : v/u - 1 - (1/u - 1/v) - u * (1/u - 1/v) = (v - u - 1) * (v - u) / (u * v) := by
    field_simp; ring
  have hneg : (v - u - 1) * (v - u) / (u * v) ≤ 0 :=
    div_nonpos_of_nonpos_of_nonneg (mul_nonpos_of_nonpos_of_nonneg (by linarith) (by linarith))
      (by positivity)
  have hd : 0 ≤ 1/u - 1/v := by
    rw [sub_nonneg]; exact one_div_le_one_div_of_le hu huv.le
  refine ⟨by linarith, by linarith, hd⟩

/-- Mertens' second theorem: `∑_{p ≤ n} 1/p = log log n + O(1)`. -/
private theorem u_mertens_second :
    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ n : ℕ, 3 ≤ n →
      |(∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, (1 : ℝ) / p) - Real.log (Real.log n)| ≤ C := by
  obtain ⟨C, hC⟩ := u_mertens_first
  have hl2 : 0 < Real.log 2 := Real.log_pos (by norm_num)
  have hC0 : 0 ≤ C := le_trans (abs_nonneg _) (hC 2 le_rfl)
  refine ⟨2 + |Real.log (Real.log 2)| + (1 + 2 * C) / Real.log 2, fun n hn => ?_⟩
  have hn2 : 2 ≤ n := by omega
  have hn' : (2:ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn2
  have hln : Real.log 2 ≤ Real.log n := Real.log_le_log (by norm_num) hn'
  have hab := ms_abel n hn2
  have hD : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n, (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1))
      = 1 / Real.log 2 - 1 / Real.log n := by
    calc _ = ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n,
          ((fun k : ℕ => -(1 / Real.log k)) (k+1) - (fun k : ℕ => -(1 / Real.log k)) k) :=
            Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun k _ => by push_cast; ring)
      _ = _ := ms_tele (fun k : ℕ => -(1 / Real.log k)) n hn2
      _ = _ := by push_cast; ring
  have hLL : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n,
        (Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k))
      = Real.log (Real.log n) - Real.log (Real.log 2) := by
    calc _ = ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n,
          ((fun k : ℕ => Real.log (Real.log k)) (k+1) - (fun k : ℕ => Real.log (Real.log k)) k) :=
            Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun k _ => by push_cast; ring)
      _ = _ := ms_tele (fun k : ℕ => Real.log (Real.log k)) n hn2
      _ = _ := by push_cast; ring
  -- per-term bounds on the Abel sum
  have hS_up : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n, (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic k).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) *
          (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1)) ≤
        ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n, ((Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k))
          + C * (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1))) := by
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum
    intro k hk
    have hk2 : 2 ≤ k := (Finset.mem_Ico.1 hk).1
    obtain ⟨_, h2, h3⟩ := ms_point k hk2
    have hA := abs_le.1 (hC k hk2)
    nlinarith
  have hS_lo : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n, ((Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k))
          - (1 + C) * (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1))) ≤
        ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico 2 n, (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic k).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) *
          (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1)) := by
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum
    intro k hk
    have hk2 : 2 ≤ k := (Finset.mem_Ico.1 hk).1
    obtain ⟨h1, _, h3⟩ := ms_point k hk2
    have hA := abs_le.1 (hC k hk2)
    nlinarith
  rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hLL, hD] at hS_up
  rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hLL, hD] at hS_lo
  -- the boundary term
  have hAn := abs_le.1 (hC n hn2)
  have hlnpos : 0 < Real.log n := by linarith
  have hB1 : (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) / Real.log n ≤ 1 + C / Real.log 2 := by
    rw [div_le_iff₀ hlnpos]
    have : C / Real.log 2 * Real.log n ≥ C := by
      rw [ge_iff_le, div_mul_eq_mul_div, le_div_iff₀ hl2]; nlinarith
    nlinarith
  have hB2 : 1 - C / Real.log 2 ≤ (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) / Real.log n := by
    rw [le_div_iff₀ hlnpos]
    have : C / Real.log 2 * Real.log n ≥ C := by
      rw [ge_iff_le, div_mul_eq_mul_div, le_div_iff₀ hl2]; nlinarith
    nlinarith
  have hDle : 1 / Real.log 2 - 1 / Real.log n ≤ 1 / Real.log 2 := by
    have : 0 < 1 / Real.log n := by positivity
    linarith
  have hD0 : 0 ≤ 1 / Real.log 2 - 1 / Real.log n := by
    rw [sub_nonneg]; exact one_div_le_one_div_of_le hl2 hln
  have hCD : C * (1 / Real.log 2 - 1 / Real.log n) ≤ C / Real.log 2 := by
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hDle hC0; rw [mul_one_div] at this; exact this
  have hCD' : (1 + C) * (1 / Real.log 2 - 1 / Real.log n) ≤ (1 + C) / Real.log 2 := by
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hDle (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 1 + C)
    rw [mul_one_div] at this; exact this
  have e : (1 + 2 * C) / Real.log 2 = 1 / Real.log 2 + C / Real.log 2 + C / Real.log 2 := by ring
  have e' : (1 + C) / Real.log 2 = 1 / Real.log 2 + C / Real.log 2 := by ring
  have hll2 := abs_le.1 (le_refl |Real.log (Real.log 2)|)
  have h12 : 0 ≤ 1 / Real.log 2 := by positivity
  rw [hab, abs_le]; constructor
  · nlinarith
  · nlinarith


private theorem ms_tele' (F : ℕ → ℝ) (m n : ℕ) (hmn : m ≤ n) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n, (F (k+1) - F k) = F n - F m := by
  induction n, hmn using Nat.le_induction with
  | base => simp
  | succ n hn ih => rw [Finset.sum_Ico_succ_top hn, ih]; ring

/-- Mertens' second theorem with the error `O(1 / log m)` on a range `(m, n]`:
`∑_{m < p ≤ n} 1/p = log log n - log log m + O(1 / log m)`. Equivalent to the classical
form `∑_{p ≤ n} 1/p = log log n + M + O(1 / log n)` (it says the left side minus `log log n`
is Cauchy at rate `1 / log m`), and directly gives the dyadic sums `∑_{P/2 < p ≤ P} 1/p`. -/
private theorem u_mertens_second_interval : ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ m n : ℕ, 2 ≤ m → m ≤ n →
    |((∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic n).filter Nat.Prime, (1 : ℝ) / p)
        - ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic m).filter Nat.Prime, (1 : ℝ) / p)
      - (Real.log (Real.log n) - Real.log (Real.log m))| ≤ C / Real.log m := by
  obtain ⟨C, hC⟩ := u_mertens_first
  have hC0 : 0 ≤ C := le_trans (abs_nonneg _) (hC 2 le_rfl)
  refine ⟨3 * C + 1, fun m n hm hmn => ?_⟩
  have hn2 : 2 ≤ n := le_trans hm hmn
  have hm' : (2:ℝ) ≤ m := by exact_mod_cast hm
  have hmn' : (m:ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hmn
  have hlm : 0 < Real.log m := Real.log_pos (by linarith)
  have hlmn : Real.log m ≤ Real.log n := Real.log_le_log (by linarith) hmn'
  have hln : 0 < Real.log n := by linarith
  have habn := ms_abel n hn2
  have habm := ms_abel m hm
  have hsplit := Finset.sum_Ico_consecutive
    (fun k : ℕ => (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic k).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) *
          (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1))) hm hmn
  have hD : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n, (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1))
      = 1 / Real.log m - 1 / Real.log n := by
    calc _ = ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n,
          ((fun k : ℕ => -(1 / Real.log k)) (k+1) - (fun k : ℕ => -(1 / Real.log k)) k) :=
            Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun k _ => by push_cast; ring)
      _ = _ := ms_tele' (fun k : ℕ => -(1 / Real.log k)) m n hmn
      _ = _ := by ring
  have hLL : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n,
        (Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k))
      = Real.log (Real.log n) - Real.log (Real.log m) := by
    calc _ = ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n,
          ((fun k : ℕ => Real.log (Real.log k)) (k+1) - (fun k : ℕ => Real.log (Real.log k)) k) :=
            Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun k _ => by push_cast; ring)
      _ = _ := ms_tele' (fun k : ℕ => Real.log (Real.log k)) m n hmn
  have hS_up : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n, (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic k).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) *
          (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1)) ≤
        ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n, ((Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k))
          + C * (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1))) := by
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum
    intro k hk
    have hk2 : 2 ≤ k := le_trans hm (Finset.mem_Ico.1 hk).1
    obtain ⟨_, h2, h3⟩ := ms_point k hk2
    have hA := abs_le.1 (hC k hk2)
    nlinarith
  have hS_lo : ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n, ((Real.log (Real.log ((k:ℝ)+1)) - Real.log (Real.log k))
          - (1 + C) * (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1))) ≤
        ∑ k ∈ Finset.Ico m n, (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic k).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) *
          (1 / Real.log k - 1 / Real.log ((k:ℝ) + 1)) := by
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum
    intro k hk
    have hk2 : 2 ≤ k := le_trans hm (Finset.mem_Ico.1 hk).1
    obtain ⟨h1, _, h3⟩ := ms_point k hk2
    have hA := abs_le.1 (hC k hk2)
    nlinarith
  rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hLL, hD] at hS_up
  rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hLL, hD] at hS_lo
  -- boundary terms: A(j)/log j = 1 + R(j)/log j with |R(j)/log j| ≤ C / log m
  have hb : ∀ j : ℕ, m ≤ j →
      |(∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic j).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) / Real.log j - 1|
        ≤ C / Real.log m := by
    intro j hj
    have hj2 : 2 ≤ j := le_trans hm hj
    have hlj : Real.log m ≤ Real.log j :=
      Real.log_le_log (by linarith) (by exact_mod_cast hj)
    have hljpos : 0 < Real.log j := by linarith
    have e : (∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic j).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) / Real.log j - 1
        = ((∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic j).filter Nat.Prime, Real.log p / p) - Real.log j) / Real.log j := by
      field_simp
    rw [e, abs_div, abs_of_pos hljpos]
    calc _ ≤ C / Real.log j := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (hC j hj2) hljpos.le
      _ ≤ C / Real.log m := div_le_div_of_nonneg_left hC0 hlm hlj
  have hbn := abs_le.1 (hb n hmn)
  have hbm := abs_le.1 (hb m le_rfl)
  have hDle : 1 / Real.log m - 1 / Real.log n ≤ 1 / Real.log m := by
    have : 0 < 1 / Real.log n := by positivity
    linarith
  have hD0 : 0 ≤ 1 / Real.log m - 1 / Real.log n := by
    rw [sub_nonneg]; exact one_div_le_one_div_of_le hlm hlmn
  have hCD : C * (1 / Real.log m - 1 / Real.log n) ≤ C / Real.log m := by
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hDle hC0; rw [mul_one_div] at this; exact this
  have hCD' : (1 + C) * (1 / Real.log m - 1 / Real.log n) ≤ (1 + C) / Real.log m := by
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hDle (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 1 + C)
    rw [mul_one_div] at this; exact this
  have e1 : (3 * C + 1) / Real.log m = C / Real.log m + C / Real.log m + (1 + C) / Real.log m := by
    ring
  have h1m : 0 ≤ C / Real.log m := by positivity
  rw [habn, habm, ← hsplit, abs_le]; constructor
  · nlinarith
  · nlinarith

/-- If at most one cell `(k, ε)` has `p ∣ n + r_{ε,k+K+1}`, the phase `X_p(n)` of (5.13)
is at most `q / 2^(K+1)` in size: the bound (5.36) behind (5.19). -/
private theorem w_X_le {K : ℕ} (q : ℝ) (hq : 0 ≤ q) (H : ℕ) (sgn : (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ)
    (hs : ∀ ε, |sgn ε| ≤ 1) (r : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (n : ℤ)
    (huniq : ∀ k1 < H, ∀ k2 < H, ∀ ε1 ε2 : Fin K → Bool,
      p ∣ (n + r ε1 (k1 + K + 1)).toNat → p ∣ (n + r ε2 (k2 + K + 1)).toNat → k1 = k2 ∧ ε1 = ε2) :
    |q * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range H, (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1)|
      ≤ q / 2 ^ (K + 1) := by
  rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg hq, div_eq_mul_inv q]
  refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hq
  by_cases hex : ∃ k < H, ∃ ε : Fin K → Bool, p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat
  · obtain ⟨k0, hk0, ε0, hd0⟩ := hex
    have hinner : ∀ k ∈ Finset.range H, k ≠ k0 →
        (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
          (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1) = 0 := by
      intro k hk hne
      rw [div_eq_zero_iff]; left
      refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun ε _ => ?_
      rw [if_neg, mul_zero]
      intro hd
      exact hne (huniq k (Finset.mem_range.mp hk) k0 hk0 ε ε0 hd hd0).1
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single k0 hinner (fun h => absurd (Finset.mem_range.mpr hk0) h)]
    have hsingle : ∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k0 + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0) = sgn ε0 := by
      rw [Finset.sum_eq_single ε0]
      · rw [if_pos hd0, mul_one]
      · intro ε _ hne
        rw [if_neg, mul_zero]
        intro hd
        exact hne (huniq k0 hk0 k0 hk0 ε ε0 hd hd0).2
      · intro h; exact absurd (Finset.mem_univ ε0) h
    rw [hsingle, abs_div, abs_of_pos (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) < 2 ^ (k0 + K + 1))]
    calc |sgn ε0| / 2 ^ (k0 + K + 1) ≤ 1 / 2 ^ (k0 + K + 1) :=
          div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (hs ε0) (by positivity)
      _ ≤ 1 / 2 ^ (K + 1) :=
          div_le_div_of_nonneg_left zero_le_one (by positivity)
            (pow_le_pow_right₀ (by norm_num) (by omega))
      _ = (2 ^ (K + 1) : ℝ)⁻¹ := one_div _
  · push Not at hex
    have hz : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range H, (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1) = 0 := by
      refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun k hk => ?_
      rw [div_eq_zero_iff]; left
      refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun ε _ => ?_
      rw [if_neg (hex k (Finset.mem_range.mp hk) ε), mul_zero]
    rw [hz, abs_zero]; positivity

/-- No collisions: if `p` divides none of the differences of two distinct shifts, and the
shifted numbers are positive, at most one cell is divisible by `p`. -/
private theorem w_uniq_of_no_collision {K : ℕ} (H : ℕ) (r : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ → ℤ) (p : ℕ)
    (n : ℤ) (hpos : ∀ ε, ∀ k < H, 0 < n + r ε (k + K + 1))
    (hcoll : ∀ k1 < H, ∀ k2 < H, ∀ ε1 ε2 : Fin K → Bool, (k1 ≠ k2 ∨ ε1 ≠ ε2) →
      ¬ (p : ℤ) ∣ r ε1 (k1 + K + 1) - r ε2 (k2 + K + 1)) :
    ∀ k1 < H, ∀ k2 < H, ∀ ε1 ε2 : Fin K → Bool,
      p ∣ (n + r ε1 (k1 + K + 1)).toNat → p ∣ (n + r ε2 (k2 + K + 1)).toNat → k1 = k2 ∧ ε1 = ε2 := by
  intro k1 hk1 k2 hk2 ε1 ε2 hd1 hd2
  by_contra hne
  rw [not_and_or] at hne
  apply hcoll k1 hk1 k2 hk2 ε1 ε2 hne
  have e1 : (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r ε1 (k1 + K + 1) := by
    have := Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mpr hd1
    rwa [Int.toNat_of_nonneg (hpos ε1 k1 hk1).le] at this
  have e2 : (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r ε2 (k2 + K + 1) := by
    have := Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mpr hd2
    rwa [Int.toNat_of_nonneg (hpos ε2 k2 hk2).le] at this
  have := dvd_sub e1 e2
  rwa [add_sub_add_left_eq_sub] at this

/-- (5.19) from the set-up, in the exact shapes of T's hole: if no prime of `S1` divides a
difference of two distinct shifts (S1 avoids the discriminant), and `q / 2^(K+1) ≤ δ`, then
`|X_p(n)| ≤ δ` on `S1 × Ns`. -/
private theorem w_5_19 (q : ℕ) (δ : ℝ) (K H : ℕ) (Ns : Finset ℤ) (S1 : Finset ℕ)
    (sgn : (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ) (r : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ → ℤ) (X : ℕ → ℤ → ℝ)
    (hsgn : ∀ ε, sgn ε = ∏ i, if ε i then (-1 : ℝ) else 1)
    (hX : ∀ p n, X p n = (q : ℝ) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range H, (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1))
    (hpos : ∀ n ∈ Ns, ∀ ε, ∀ k < H, 0 < n + r ε (k + K + 1))
    (hcoll : ∀ p ∈ S1, ∀ k1 < H, ∀ k2 < H, ∀ ε1 ε2 : Fin K → Bool, (k1 ≠ k2 ∨ ε1 ≠ ε2) →
      ¬ (p : ℤ) ∣ r ε1 (k1 + K + 1) - r ε2 (k2 + K + 1))
    (hK : (q : ℝ) / 2 ^ (K + 1) ≤ δ) :
    ∀ p ∈ S1, ∀ n ∈ Ns, |X p n| ≤ δ := by
  intro p hp n hn
  have hs : ∀ ε, |sgn ε| ≤ 1 := by
    intro ε
    rw [hsgn ε, Finset.abs_prod]
    refine le_of_eq (Finset.prod_eq_one fun i _ => ?_)
    split_ifs <;> norm_num
  rw [hX p n]
  exact (w_X_le (q : ℝ) (Nat.cast_nonneg q) H sgn hs r p n
    (w_uniq_of_no_collision H r p n (fun ε k hk => hpos n hn ε k hk) (hcoll p hp))).trans hK

/-- A sum with at most one nonzero term squares termwise. -/
private theorem w_sq_sum_of_single {ι : Type*} (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → ℝ)
    (h : ∀ i ∈ s, ∀ j ∈ s, f i ≠ 0 → f j ≠ 0 → i = j) :
    (∑ i ∈ s, f i) ^ 2 = ∑ i ∈ s, f i ^ 2 := by
  by_cases hex : ∃ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0
  · obtain ⟨i0, hi0, hf0⟩ := hex
    have hz : ∀ j ∈ s, j ≠ i0 → f j = 0 := fun j hj hne => by
      by_contra hfj; exact hne (h j hj i0 hi0 hfj hf0)
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i0 hz (fun h' => absurd hi0 h'),
      Finset.sum_eq_single i0 (fun j hj hne => by rw [hz j hj hne]; ring) (fun h' => absurd hi0 h')]
  · push Not at hex
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_zero hex, Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i hi => by rw [hex i hi]; ring]
    ring

/-- `Σ_ε (-1)^{|ε|} = 0` for `K ≥ 1`. -/
private theorem w_sum_sgn_eq_zero {K : ℕ} (hK : 1 ≤ K) (sgn : (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ)
    (hsgn : ∀ ε, sgn ε = ∏ i, if ε i then (-1 : ℝ) else 1) :
    ∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε = 0 := by
  simp_rw [hsgn]
  rw [← Fintype.piFinset_univ, ← Finset.prod_univ_sum (fun _ : Fin K => (Finset.univ : Finset Bool))
    (fun _ b => if b then (-1 : ℝ) else 1)]
  exact Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ ⟨0, hK⟩) (by simp)

/-- S8h with the paper's approximate equidistribution (5.35): if every residue class mod `p`
holds `#Ns/p` points of `Ns` up to a relative error `η ≤ 1`, then
`Var X_p ≥ (1 − η) q²/(2^(K+2) p) − (η q / p)²`. -/
private theorem w_var_lower_approx (q : ℕ) (K H : ℕ) (hK : 1 ≤ K) (hH : 1 ≤ H) (Ns : Finset ℤ)
    (hNs : Ns.Nonempty) (p : ℕ) (hp : 0 < p) (η : ℝ) (hη : 0 ≤ η) (hη1 : η ≤ 1)
    (sgn : (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ) (r : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ → ℤ) (X : ℕ → ℤ → ℝ)
    (hsgn : ∀ ε, sgn ε = ∏ i, if ε i then (-1 : ℝ) else 1)
    (hX : ∀ p n, X p n = (q : ℝ) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range H, (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1))
    (hpos : ∀ n ∈ Ns, ∀ ε, ∀ k < H, 0 < n + r ε (k + K + 1))
    (hcoll : ∀ k1 < H, ∀ k2 < H, ∀ ε1 ε2 : Fin K → Bool, (k1 ≠ k2 ∨ ε1 ≠ ε2) →
      ¬ (p : ℤ) ∣ r ε1 (k1 + K + 1) - r ε2 (k2 + K + 1))
    (hequi : ∀ a : ℤ, |((Ns.filter fun n => (p : ℤ) ∣ n + a).card : ℝ) * p - Ns.card|
      ≤ η * Ns.card) :
    (1 - η) * ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / (2 ^ (K + 2) * p)) - (η * q / p) ^ 2 ≤
      (Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n ^ 2 - ((Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n) ^ 2 := by
  set C := Finset.range H ×ˢ (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin K → Bool)) with hC
  set a : ℕ × (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ := fun c => (q : ℝ) * sgn c.2 / 2 ^ (c.1 + K + 1) with ha
  set ind : ℤ → ℕ × (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ :=
    fun n c => if (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r c.2 (c.1 + K + 1) then 1 else 0 with hind
  set N : ℝ := (Ns.card : ℝ) with hN
  have hNpos : 0 < N := by rw [hN]; exact_mod_cast hNs.card_pos
  have hpR : (0 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp
  have hdv : ∀ z : ℤ, 0 ≤ z → (p ∣ z.toNat ↔ (p : ℤ) ∣ z) := fun z hz => by
    rw [← Int.natCast_dvd_natCast, Int.toNat_of_nonneg hz]
  have hXc : ∀ n ∈ Ns, X p n = ∑ c ∈ C, a c * ind n c := by
    intro n hn
    rw [hX, hC, Finset.sum_product, Finset.mul_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k hk => ?_
    rw [Finset.sum_div, Finset.mul_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ε _ => ?_
    have hz := (hpos n hn ε k (Finset.mem_range.mp hk)).le
    simp only [ha, hind]
    by_cases h : (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r ε (k + K + 1)
    · rw [if_pos h, if_pos ((hdv _ hz).mpr h)]; ring
    · rw [if_neg h, if_neg (fun h' => h ((hdv _ hz).mp h'))]; ring
  have hcnt : ∀ c : ℕ × (Fin K → Bool), |(∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c) - N / p| ≤ η * N / p := by
    intro c
    have h := hequi (r c.2 (c.1 + K + 1))
    have hs : ∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c = ((Ns.filter fun n => (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r c.2 (c.1 + K + 1)).card : ℝ) := by
      simp only [hind]; rw [Finset.sum_boole]
    rw [hs]
    have e : ((Ns.filter fun n => (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r c.2 (c.1 + K + 1)).card : ℝ) - N / p
        = (((Ns.filter fun n => (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r c.2 (c.1 + K + 1)).card : ℝ) * p - N) / p := by
      field_simp
    rw [e, abs_div, abs_of_pos hpR]
    exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_right h hpR.le
  have hsq : ∀ n ∈ Ns, X p n ^ 2 = ∑ c ∈ C, a c ^ 2 * ind n c := by
    intro n hn
    rw [hXc n hn, w_sq_sum_of_single]
    · refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun c _ => ?_
      simp only [hind]; split_ifs <;> ring
    · intro c hc c' hc' h1 h2
      have d1 : (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r c.2 (c.1 + K + 1) := by
        by_contra h; apply h1; simp only [hind, if_neg h, mul_zero]
      have d2 : (p : ℤ) ∣ n + r c'.2 (c'.1 + K + 1) := by
        by_contra h; apply h2; simp only [hind, if_neg h, mul_zero]
      by_contra hne
      have hor : c.1 ≠ c'.1 ∨ c.2 ≠ c'.2 := by
        by_contra h; push Not at h; exact hne (Prod.ext h.1 h.2)
      rw [hC, Finset.mem_product] at hc hc'
      apply hcoll c.1 (Finset.mem_range.mp hc.1) c'.1 (Finset.mem_range.mp hc'.1) c.2 c'.2 hor
      have := dvd_sub d1 d2
      rwa [add_sub_add_left_eq_sub] at this
  -- Σ_c a c = 0 and Σ_c |a c| ≤ q
  have hsuma : ∑ c ∈ C, a c = 0 := by
    rw [hC, Finset.sum_product]
    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun k _ => ?_
    simp only [ha]
    rw [← Finset.sum_div, ← Finset.mul_sum, w_sum_sgn_eq_zero hK sgn hsgn]; ring
  have habs : ∑ c ∈ C, |a c| ≤ q := by
    have hs1 : ∀ ε : Fin K → Bool, |sgn ε| = 1 := by
      intro ε; rw [hsgn ε, Finset.abs_prod]
      exact Finset.prod_eq_one fun i _ => by split_ifs <;> norm_num
    rw [hC, Finset.sum_product]
    simp only [ha, abs_div, abs_mul, hs1, Nat.abs_cast, mul_one, abs_pow, abs_two]
    simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul, Fintype.card_fun,
      Fintype.card_bool, Fintype.card_fin]
    have hk : ∀ k ∈ Finset.range H, ((2 ^ K : ℕ) : ℝ) * ((q : ℝ) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1))
        = (q : ℝ) * ((1 / 2) * (1 / 2) ^ k) := by
      intro k _; push_cast; rw [pow_add, pow_add]; field_simp
      rw [mul_assoc, ← mul_pow]; norm_num
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hk, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.mul_sum]
    have hg := sum_geometric_two_le H
    have hq0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ q := Nat.cast_nonneg q
    nlinarith
  have hmean : |(N)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n| ≤ η * q / p := by
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hXc, Finset.sum_comm]
    simp_rw [← Finset.mul_sum]
    have e : ∑ c ∈ C, a c * ∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c = ∑ c ∈ C, a c * ((∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c) - N / p) := by
      simp_rw [mul_sub]; rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.sum_mul, hsuma]; ring
    rw [e, abs_mul, abs_inv, abs_of_pos hNpos]
    have hb : |∑ c ∈ C, a c * ((∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c) - N / p)| ≤ q * (η * N / p) := by
      refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans ?_
      calc ∑ c ∈ C, |a c * ((∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c) - N / p)|
          ≤ ∑ c ∈ C, |a c| * (η * N / p) := Finset.sum_le_sum fun c _ => by
            rw [abs_mul]; exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hcnt c) (abs_nonneg _)
        _ = (∑ c ∈ C, |a c|) * (η * N / p) := by rw [Finset.sum_mul]
        _ ≤ q * (η * N / p) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right habs (by positivity)
    calc N⁻¹ * |∑ c ∈ C, a c * ((∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c) - N / p)| ≤ N⁻¹ * (q * (η * N / p)) :=
          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hb (by positivity)
      _ = η * q / p := by field_simp
  have hsecond : (1 - η) * (N / p) * ∑ c ∈ C, a c ^ 2 ≤ ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n ^ 2 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hsq, Finset.sum_comm]
    simp_rw [← Finset.mul_sum]
    rw [Finset.mul_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun c _ => ?_
    have h := hcnt c
    rw [abs_le] at h
    have : (1 - η) * (N / p) ≤ ∑ n ∈ Ns, ind n c := by
      have : (1 - η) * (N / p) = N / p - η * N / p := by ring
      linarith [h.1]
    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a c)]
  have hlow : (q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 ^ (K + 2) ≤ ∑ c ∈ C, a c ^ 2 := by
    have hsub : ({0} : Finset ℕ) ×ˢ (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin K → Bool)) ⊆ C := by
      rw [hC]; exact Finset.product_subset_product_left
        (Finset.singleton_subset_iff.mpr (Finset.mem_range.mpr hH))
    refine le_trans (le_of_eq ?_) (Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg hsub
      fun c _ _ => sq_nonneg _)
    rw [Finset.sum_product, Finset.sum_singleton]
    have hs2 : ∀ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε ^ 2 = 1 := by
      intro ε
      rw [hsgn ε, ← Finset.prod_pow]
      exact Finset.prod_eq_one fun i _ => by split_ifs <;> norm_num
    simp only [ha, div_pow, mul_pow, hs2, mul_one]
    rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul]
    simp only [Fintype.card_fun, Fintype.card_bool, Fintype.card_fin, zero_add]
    push_cast
    rw [pow_succ 2 (K + 1), pow_succ 2 K]
    field_simp
  have hEX2 : (1 - η) * ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / (2 ^ (K + 2) * p)) ≤ N⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n ^ 2 := by
    have h1 : (1 - η) * ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / (2 ^ (K + 2) * p))
        = N⁻¹ * ((1 - η) * (N / p) * ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 ^ (K + 2))) := by
      field_simp
    rw [h1]
    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_trans ?_ hsecond) (by positivity)
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hlow (mul_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity))
  have hEX : (N⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n) ^ 2 ≤ (η * q / p) ^ 2 := by
    rw [← sq_abs]
    exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) hmean 2
  linarith

/-- (5.21) from the set-up: summing `w_var_lower_approx` over `S1`. If every `p ∈ S1` avoids the
shifts' discriminant and `n` is equidistributed mod `p` up to relative error `η ≤ 1`, then
`Σ_{S1} Var X_p ≥ (1-η) q²/2^(K+2) Σ_{S1} 1/p − η² q² Σ_{S1} 1/p²`; the hole's `c0 ≤ Σ Var`
follows as soon as the right side is `≥ c0` (by Mertens, `Σ_{S1} 1/p ≍ log₂ R − log₂ W`). -/
private theorem w_5_21 (q : ℕ) (K H : ℕ) (hK : 1 ≤ K) (hH : 1 ≤ H) (Ns : Finset ℤ)
    (hNs : Ns.Nonempty) (S1 : Finset ℕ) (hS1 : ∀ p ∈ S1, 0 < p) (η : ℝ) (hη : 0 ≤ η)
    (hη1 : η ≤ 1) (sgn : (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ) (r : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ → ℤ) (X : ℕ → ℤ → ℝ)
    (hsgn : ∀ ε, sgn ε = ∏ i, if ε i then (-1 : ℝ) else 1)
    (hX : ∀ p n, X p n = (q : ℝ) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range H, (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1))
    (hpos : ∀ n ∈ Ns, ∀ ε, ∀ k < H, 0 < n + r ε (k + K + 1))
    (hcoll : ∀ p ∈ S1, ∀ k1 < H, ∀ k2 < H, ∀ ε1 ε2 : Fin K → Bool, (k1 ≠ k2 ∨ ε1 ≠ ε2) →
      ¬ (p : ℤ) ∣ r ε1 (k1 + K + 1) - r ε2 (k2 + K + 1))
    (hequi : ∀ p ∈ S1, ∀ a : ℤ, |((Ns.filter fun n => (p : ℤ) ∣ n + a).card : ℝ) * p - Ns.card|
      ≤ η * Ns.card) (c0 : ℝ)
    (hc0 : c0 ≤ (1 - η) * ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 ^ (K + 2)) * ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p
      - η ^ 2 * (q : ℝ) ^ 2 * ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p ^ 2) :
    c0 ≤ ∑ p ∈ S1, ((Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n ^ 2
        - ((Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, X p n) ^ 2) := by
  refine hc0.trans ?_
  rw [Finset.mul_sum, Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun p hp => le_trans (le_of_eq ?_)
    (w_var_lower_approx q K H hK hH Ns hNs p (hS1 p hp) η hη hη1 sgn r X hsgn hX hpos
      (hcoll p hp) (hequi p hp))
  have : (0 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hS1 p hp
  field_simp

/-- One vertex of κ₁ (§5.7): if `m n ε` is equidistributed mod `P ε` on `Ns` up to a factor
`1 + η`, the average of `Σ_k 1[P² ∣ P m + P (k+1)] / 2^(k+1)` is at most `(1 + η)/P`. -/
private theorem w_kappa1_eps (Ns : Finset ℤ) (P : ℕ) (hP : 0 < P) (m : ℤ → ℕ) (η : ℝ)
    (hequi : ∀ a : ℕ, ((Ns.filter fun n => P ∣ m n + a).card : ℝ) * P ≤ (1 + η) * Ns.card) :
    ∑ n ∈ Ns, ∑' k : ℕ, (if P ^ 2 ∣ P * m n + P * (k + 1) then (1 : ℝ) else 0) / 2 ^ (k + 1)
      ≤ (1 + η) * Ns.card / P := by
  have hdv : ∀ n (k : ℕ), (P ^ 2 ∣ P * m n + P * (k + 1) ↔ P ∣ m n + (k + 1)) := by
    intro n k
    rw [pow_two, ← mul_add]
    exact Nat.mul_dvd_mul_iff_left hP
  have hsum : ∀ n ∈ Ns, Summable fun k : ℕ =>
      (if P ^ 2 ∣ P * m n + P * (k + 1) then (1 : ℝ) else 0) / 2 ^ (k + 1) := by
    intro n _
    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => by positivity) (fun k => ?_)
      ((summable_geometric_two).mul_left (1 / 2))
    have : (if P ^ 2 ∣ P * m n + P * (k + 1) then (1 : ℝ) else 0) ≤ 1 := by split_ifs <;> norm_num
    calc (if P ^ 2 ∣ P * m n + P * (k + 1) then (1 : ℝ) else 0) / 2 ^ (k + 1)
        ≤ 1 / 2 ^ (k + 1) := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right this (by positivity)
      _ = 1 / 2 * (1 / 2) ^ k := by
        rw [pow_succ]; field_simp; rw [← mul_pow]; norm_num
  rw [← Summable.tsum_finsetSum hsum]
  have hPR : (0 : ℝ) < P := by exact_mod_cast hP
  have hterm : ∀ k : ℕ, ∑ n ∈ Ns,
      (if P ^ 2 ∣ P * m n + P * (k + 1) then (1 : ℝ) else 0) / 2 ^ (k + 1)
      ≤ (1 + η) * Ns.card / P * (1 / 2 * (1 / 2) ^ k) := by
    intro k
    rw [← Finset.sum_div]
    simp_rw [hdv]
    rw [Finset.sum_boole]
    have h := hequi (k + 1)
    have : ((Ns.filter fun n => P ∣ m n + (k + 1)).card : ℝ) ≤ (1 + η) * Ns.card / P := by
      rw [le_div_iff₀ hPR]; exact_mod_cast h
    calc ((Ns.filter fun n => P ∣ m n + (k + 1)).card : ℝ) / 2 ^ (k + 1)
        ≤ (1 + η) * Ns.card / P / 2 ^ (k + 1) := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right this (by positivity)
      _ = (1 + η) * Ns.card / P * (1 / 2 * (1 / 2) ^ k) := by
        rw [pow_succ]; field_simp; rw [mul_assoc, ← mul_pow]; norm_num
  have hS : Summable fun k : ℕ => (1 + η) * Ns.card / P * (1 / 2 * (1 / 2) ^ k) :=
    ((summable_geometric_two).mul_left (1 / 2)).mul_left _
  refine (Summable.tsum_le_tsum hterm (summable_sum fun n hn => hsum n hn) hS).trans (le_of_eq ?_)
  rw [tsum_mul_left, tsum_mul_left, tsum_geometric_two]; ring

/-- κ₁ of (5.10), T's exact conjunct: with `m n ε` equidistributed mod `P ε` up to `1 + η` for
every vertex `ε`, `κ₁ ≤ Σ_ε (1 + η)/P_ε` (≤ 2^K (1+η) / min P_ε, which is `o(1)` since
`P_ε ≥ P/2` and `2^K ≈ (log₃ x)²`). -/
private theorem w_kappa1 {K : ℕ} (Ns : Finset ℤ) (P : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ) (hP : ∀ ε, 0 < P ε)
    (m : ℤ → (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ) (η : ℝ) (hη : 0 ≤ η)
    (hequi : ∀ ε, ∀ a : ℕ, ((Ns.filter fun n => P ε ∣ m n ε + a).card : ℝ) * P ε
      ≤ (1 + η) * Ns.card) :
    (Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, ∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, ∑' k : ℕ,
        (if P ε ^ 2 ∣ P ε * m n ε + P ε * (k + 1) then (1 : ℝ) else 0) / 2 ^ (k + 1)
      ≤ ∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, (1 + η) / P ε := by
  rcases Ns.eq_empty_or_nonempty with h | hne
  · subst h; simp only [Finset.card_empty, Nat.cast_zero, inv_zero, zero_mul]
    exact Finset.sum_nonneg fun ε _ => div_nonneg (by linarith) (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < Ns.card := by exact_mod_cast hne.card_pos
  rw [Finset.sum_comm, Finset.mul_sum]
  refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun ε _ => ?_
  have := w_kappa1_eps Ns (P ε) (hP ε) (fun n => m n ε) η (hequi ε)
  calc (Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, ∑' k : ℕ,
        (if P ε ^ 2 ∣ P ε * m n ε + P ε * (k + 1) then (1 : ℝ) else 0) / 2 ^ (k + 1)
      ≤ (Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ((1 + η) * Ns.card / P ε) :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this (by positivity)
    _ = (1 + η) / P ε := by field_simp

/-- `X_p(n)` depends on `n` only through `n mod p` (for positive shifted values). -/
private theorem w_X_congr {K : ℕ} (q H : ℕ) (sgn : (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ)
    (r : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ → ℤ) (X : ℕ → ℤ → ℝ)
    (hX : ∀ p n, X p n = (q : ℝ) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range H, (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1))
    (p : ℕ) (n n' : ℤ) (hn : ∀ ε, ∀ k < H, 0 < n + r ε (k + K + 1))
    (hn' : ∀ ε, ∀ k < H, 0 < n' + r ε (k + K + 1)) (hpn : (p : ℤ) ∣ n - n') :
    X p n = X p n' := by
  have hdv : ∀ z : ℤ, 0 ≤ z → (p ∣ z.toNat ↔ (p : ℤ) ∣ z) := fun z hz => by
    rw [← Int.natCast_dvd_natCast, Int.toNat_of_nonneg hz]
  rw [hX, hX]
  congr 1
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k hk => ?_
  congr 1
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun ε _ => ?_
  have hk' := Finset.mem_range.mp hk
  have e : (p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat) ↔ (p ∣ (n' + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat) := by
    rw [hdv _ (hn ε k hk').le, hdv _ (hn' ε k hk').le]
    constructor
    · intro h
      have := dvd_sub h hpn
      rwa [show n + r ε (k + K + 1) - (n - n') = n' + r ε (k + K + 1) by ring] at this
    · intro h
      have := dvd_add h hpn
      rwa [show n' + r ε (k + K + 1) + (n - n') = n + r ε (k + K + 1) by ring] at this
  by_cases h : p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat
  · rw [if_pos h, if_pos (e.mp h)]
  · rw [if_neg h, if_neg (fun h' => h (e.mpr h'))]

/-- The `S₀` conjunct of T's hole (§5.6, (5.33)): if `Ns` lies in one residue class modulo
every prime `p ≤ B` outside `S1 ∪ S2` (the paper takes `n ≡ 0 mod W♭` and fixes `n mod p_ε`
by (5.5)), then `X_p` is constant on `Ns` for those `p`. -/
private theorem w_S0 {K : ℕ} (q H B : ℕ) (Ns : Finset ℤ) (S1 S2 : Finset ℕ)
    (sgn : (Fin K → Bool) → ℝ) (r : (Fin K → Bool) → ℕ → ℤ) (X : ℕ → ℤ → ℝ)
    (hX : ∀ p n, X p n = (q : ℝ) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range H, (∑ ε : Fin K → Bool, sgn ε *
        (if p ∣ (n + r ε (k + K + 1)).toNat then (1 : ℝ) else 0)) / 2 ^ (k + K + 1))
    (hpos : ∀ n ∈ Ns, ∀ ε, ∀ k < H, 0 < n + r ε (k + K + 1))
    (hcls : ∀ p ∈ (Finset.range (B + 1)).filter Nat.Prime, p ∉ S1 → p ∉ S2 →
      ∀ n ∈ Ns, ∀ n' ∈ Ns, (p : ℤ) ∣ n - n') :
    ∀ p ∈ (Finset.range (B + 1)).filter Nat.Prime, p ∉ S1 → p ∉ S2 →
      ∀ n ∈ Ns, ∀ n' ∈ Ns, X p n = X p n' :=
  fun p hp h1 h2 n hn n' hn' => w_X_congr q H sgn r X hX p n n' (hpos n hn) (hpos n' hn')
    (hcls p hp h1 h2 n hn n' hn')

/-- `∑_{p ∈ S} 1/p² ≤ 1` for any finite set of integers `≥ 2` (from Mathlib's `sum_Ioo_inv_sq_le`). -/
private theorem f2_sum_inv_sq_le_one (S : Finset ℕ) (hS : ∀ p ∈ S, 2 ≤ p) :
    ∑ p ∈ S, (1 : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by
  have hsub : S ⊆ Finset.Ioo 1 (S.sup id + 1) := by
    intro p hp
    have h2 := hS p hp
    have hle : p ≤ S.sup id := Finset.le_sup (f := id) hp
    simp only [Finset.mem_Ioo]; omega
  calc ∑ p ∈ S, (1 : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 = ∑ p ∈ S, ((p : ℝ) ^ 2)⁻¹ := by
        simp only [one_div]
    _ ≤ ∑ p ∈ Finset.Ioo 1 (S.sup id + 1), ((p : ℝ) ^ 2)⁻¹ :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg hsub (fun _ _ _ => by positivity)
    _ ≤ 2 / ((1 : ℕ) + 1 : ℝ) := sum_Ioo_inv_sq_le 1 _
    _ = 1 := by norm_num

/-- The primes of `(w, R]` as a difference of two `Iic` sums. -/
private theorem f2_sum_Ioc (f : ℕ → ℝ) (w R : ℕ) (h : w ≤ R) :
    ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Ioc w R).filter Nat.Prime, f p
      = ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic R).filter Nat.Prime, f p - ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Iic w).filter Nat.Prime, f p := by
  have hsub : (Finset.Iic w).filter Nat.Prime ⊆ (Finset.Iic R).filter Nat.Prime := by
    intro x hx
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_Iic] at hx ⊢
    exact ⟨hx.1.trans h, hx.2⟩
  have heq : (Finset.Ioc w R).filter Nat.Prime
      = (Finset.Iic R).filter Nat.Prime \ (Finset.Iic w).filter Nat.Prime := by
    ext x
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_Ioc, Finset.mem_Iic, Finset.mem_sdiff]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨⟨h1, h2⟩, hp⟩
      exact ⟨⟨h2, hp⟩, fun h3 => by omega⟩
    · rintro ⟨⟨h2, hp⟩, h3⟩
      refine ⟨⟨?_, h2⟩, hp⟩
      by_contra h4
      exact h3 ⟨by omega, hp⟩
  rw [heq, ← Finset.sum_sdiff hsub]
  ring

/-- `S1` keeps all but a reciprocal mass `≤ 1` of the primes of `(w, R]`. -/
private theorem f2_S1_lower (S1 : Finset ℕ) (w R : ℕ)
    (hmiss : ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Ioc w R).filter Nat.Prime \ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p ≤ 1) :
    ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Ioc w R).filter Nat.Prime, (1 : ℝ) / p - 1 ≤ ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p := by
  set A := (Finset.Ioc w R).filter Nat.Prime
  have h1 : ∑ p ∈ A, (1 : ℝ) / p ≤ ∑ p ∈ (A \ S1) ∪ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p :=
    Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg (fun x hx => by
      by_cases hxs : x ∈ S1
      · exact Finset.mem_union_right _ hxs
      · exact Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_sdiff.mpr ⟨hx, hxs⟩))
      (fun _ _ _ => by positivity)
  rw [Finset.sum_union Finset.sdiff_disjoint] at h1
  linarith

/-- Mertens' second theorem on `S1` gives the variance conjunct: with `c0 = q²/8`. -/
private theorem f2_c0 (q K : ℕ) (S1 : Finset ℕ) (hS1 : ∀ p ∈ S1, 2 ≤ p) (η : ℝ)
    (hη0 : 0 ≤ η) (hη : η ≤ 1 / 4) (C : ℝ) (hC : 0 ≤ C)
    (hs : (2 : ℝ) ^ K * (C + 2) - C - 1 ≤ ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p) :
    (q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 8 ≤ (1 - η) * ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 ^ (K + 2)) * ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p
      - η ^ 2 * (q : ℝ) ^ 2 * ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p ^ 2 := by
  have hsq := f2_sum_inv_sq_le_one S1 hS1
  have hsq0 : 0 ≤ ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 := Finset.sum_nonneg (fun _ _ => by positivity)
  have h2K : (1 : ℝ) ≤ 2 ^ K := one_le_pow₀ (by norm_num)
  set S := ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p
  have hS : (2 : ℝ) ^ K ≤ S := by nlinarith
  have hq0 : 0 ≤ (q : ℝ) ^ 2 := sq_nonneg _
  have hpos : (0 : ℝ) < 2 ^ (K + 2) := by positivity
  have hA : (q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 4 ≤ ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 ^ (K + 2)) * S := by
    rw [div_mul_eq_mul_div, le_div_iff₀ hpos, pow_add]
    nlinarith
  have hB : η ^ 2 * (q : ℝ) ^ 2 * ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 ≤ (q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 16 := by
    have : η ^ 2 ≤ 1 / 16 := by nlinarith
    calc η ^ 2 * (q : ℝ) ^ 2 * ∑ p ∈ S1, (1 : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 ≤ η ^ 2 * (q : ℝ) ^ 2 * 1 :=
          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsq (by positivity)
      _ ≤ 1 / 16 * (q : ℝ) ^ 2 * 1 := by gcongr
      _ = (q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 16 := by ring
  have hC' : 0 ≤ ((q : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 ^ (K + 2)) * S := by positivity
  nlinarith

/-- `log w ≥ 1` once `w ≥ 3`. -/
private theorem f2_one_le_log (w : ℕ) (hw : 3 ≤ w) : 1 ≤ Real.log w := by
  have h3 : (3 : ℝ) ≤ w := by exact_mod_cast hw
  rw [Real.le_log_iff_exp_le (by linarith)]
  linarith [Real.exp_one_lt_d9]

          (∏ p ∈ S1, (Ns.card : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ n ∈ Ns, Real.exp (2 * Real.pi * Real.exp 1 * X p n)

                Real.exp (-(2 * Real.pi * Real.exp 1) * X p n)) / Real.exp 1 ^ M ≤ δ) ∧
        (3 ≤ w ∧ w ≤ R ∧
          (2 : ℝ) ^ K * T ≤ Real.log (Real.log R) - Real.log (Real.log w) ∧
          ∑ p ∈ (Finset.Ioc w R).filter Nat.Prime \ S1, (1 : ℝ) / p ≤ 1) := sorry
  obtain ⟨C, hC⟩ := u_mertens_second_interval

    fun n hn ε k hk => (hrange n hn ε k hk).1

$\displaystyle \forall q \ge 1\ \exists c_0 > 0\ \forall \delta > 0:\ \text{a prime cube } p_\varepsilon = p_0 + \textstyle\sum_k \varepsilon_k v_k, \text{ a finite set of } n \text{ obeying (5.5)--(5.6), a split } S_0 \sqcup S_1 \sqcup S_2 \text{ of the primes} \le B \text{ with } X_p \text{ constant on } S_0,\ |X_p| \le \delta \text{ on } S_1,\ M \text{ even},\ \kappa_1, \dots, \kappa_5 \le \delta,\ \sum_{p \in S_1} \operatorname{Var} X_p \ge c_0$

Theorem 5.1 of arXiv:2512.01739v2 (p. 49) for n uniform on the finite set Ns, where X_p(n) = q sum_{1<=h<=H} sum_eps (-1)^|eps| 1[p | n + r_{eps,h+K}] / 2^(h+K) is (5.13), and kappa_1, kappa_2, kappa_3 are (5.10), (5.11), (5.14) as printed (expectation and sums swapped). kappa_4, kappa_5 are (5.16), (5.17) as printed, with X'_p independent copies (n uniform on Ns^{S_1}) and e = exp 1 in (5.17). One part is taken from the paper's proof rather than its statement: (5.19), |X_p| small on S_1 (shown in section 5.6 from (5.36)). It does not mention the sum or rationality: it is about prime factors of shifted integers, and in the paper its proof rests on the paper's Theorem 3.1 and on Lemma 5.2.

Restated as hole h_two_chains of erdos-257 (split by 20261023T133841Z-t1023-f1-partial.lean).

What is claimed

$\displaystyle \forall\, x, y : \mathbb{N} \to \mathbb{N},\quad \Big(x_0, y_0 > 0,\ \ x_j \mid x_{j+1},\ y_j \mid y_{j+1},\ 3x_j \le x_{j+1},\ 3y_j \le y_{j+1}\ \text{for all } j\Big) \;\Longrightarrow\; \sum_{n \in \{x_j\} \cup \{y_j\}} \frac{1}{2^n - 1} \notin \mathbb{Q}.$

The sum is over the set of values of the two sequences, each value counted once. Each sequence is a divisibility chain of positive integers growing at least threefold per step, so every element is at least $1$ and no term meets Lean's junk value at $n = 0$.

What is claimed

$\displaystyle \forall\, k \ge 1:\qquad \sum_{m \in \{n^k \,:\, n \in \mathbb{N}\}} \frac{1}{2^m-1} \ \text{ is irrational}.$

The set of perfect $k$-th powers includes $0 = 0^k$ and $1$. Sums are Lean's tsum over the set, the ordinary sum of a convergent nonnegative series; the term at $m = 0$ is Lean's $\frac{1}{2^0-1} = \frac10 = 0$.

(Written by t1022-e; filed by t1022-k.)

Selected statement every arithmetic progression (Erdős 1948, Borwein 1991)

variant-886b66daRename or correct →

status
proved
attempts
0 attempts

Restated as hole h_progressions of erdos-257 (split by 20261023T133841Z-t1023-f1-partial.lean).

What is claimed

$\displaystyle \forall\, a, d \in \mathbb{N},\ d \ge 1:\qquad \sum_{m \in \{a + dn \,:\, n \ge 0\}} \frac{1}{2^m-1} \ \text{ is irrational}.$

Every infinite arithmetic progression with first term $a \ge 0$ and difference $d \ge 1$. Sums are Lean's tsum over the set, the ordinary sum of a convergent nonnegative series; the term at $m = 0$ is Lean's $\frac{1}{2^0-1} = \frac10 = 0$.

    ∀ a d : ℕ, 0 < d →
      Irrational (∑' m : {m : ℕ | ∃ n, m = a + d * n}, (1 : ℝ) / (2 ^ m.1 - 1)) := by

How it is being proved

Proved by Proof.lean (merged in feb5385fd306) · attestation 000873.

What is known

Literature: Known: a proof is published and not formalised · proposed by t1022-d, awaiting a steward or curator

References and summary
Literature status

Proposed by t1022-d, awaiting a steward or curator: Known: a proof is published and not formalised (record, 2026-10-22T13:56:19Z).

  • P. Erdős, On arithmetical properties of Lambert series, J. Indian Math. Soc. (N.S.) 12 (1948), 63–66 — Theorem (p. 63): f(1/t) = Σ_{n≥1} 1/(t^n − 1) is irrational for every integer |t| > 1; the proof makes long zero blocks in the base-t expansion of Σ d(n)/t^n by a Chinese-remainder choice of consecutive integers with many divisors.
  • P. B. Borwein, On the irrationality of Σ 1/(q^n + r), J. Number Theory 37 (1991), 253–259 — As stated by Kovač–Tao (arXiv:2406.17593, §2.1.2 p. 4): Σ_{n≥1} 1/(t^n + q) is irrational for integer t ≥ 2 and rational q ∉ {0, −t^n}. With t = 2^d, q = −2^(−a): 1/(2^(a+dn) − 1) = 2^(−a)/(t^n + q).
  • F. Luca and Y. Tachiya, Irrationality of Lambert series associated with a periodic sequence, Int. J. Number Theory 10 (2014), 623–636, Theorem 1.1 — Σ_{n≥1} a(n)/(q^n − 1) is irrational for every purely periodic integer sequence a, not identically zero, and every integer |q| > 1. Read in the authors' restatement as Theorem A, RIMS Kôkyûroku 2014 (2017), p. 139 (kurims.kyoto-u.ac.jp/~kyodo/kokyuroku/contents/pdf/2014-14.pdf).

Untrusted contributor text — a literature summary (D-25 v3.35): the contributor’s reading, not a verdict.

Untrusted: summary by t1022-d, drafted with Claude Opus 5.5 (Claude Code); papers read 2026-10-22 from the linked PDFs. Rendered from targets/erdos-257/nodes/variant-886b66da/literature/20261022T135619Z-t1022-d.yaml.

Known. a = 0 is Erdős 1948 at base 2^d; a ≥ 1 follows from Borwein 1991 after the rescaling in the note (the n = 0 term is rational); all cases also follow from Luca–Tachiya 2014, Theorem 1.1, since an arithmetic progression has a periodic indicator. A Lean proof (Aristotle via agent C, using the periodic case) was submitted on 2026-10-22 as graph PR #873.

Selected statement union of finitely many lacunary sequences

variant-90827f46Rename or correct →

status
proved
attempts
0 attempts

Restated as hole h_lacunary_union of erdos-257 (split by 20261023T133841Z-t1023-f1-partial.lean).

What is claimed

$\displaystyle \forall\, r \ge 1,\ \forall\, c_0, \dots, c_{r-1} : \mathbb{N} \to \mathbb{N},\quad \Big(c_i(0) > 0,\ \ (2r+1)^{r+1}\, c_i(j) \le c_i(j+1)\ \text{for all } i, j\Big) \;\Longrightarrow\; \sum_{n \in \bigcup_i \{c_i(j) : j \in \mathbb{N}\}} \frac{1}{2^n - 1} \notin \mathbb{Q}.$

The sum is over the union of the $r$ sequences' values, each value counted once. Each sequence grows by a factor of at least $(2r+1)^{r+1}$ per step (9 for one sequence, 125 for two, 2401 for three); nothing is assumed about divisibility or about how the sequences sit relative to each other. Every element is at least $1$, so no term meets Lean's junk value at $n = 0$.

unverified · history

Lean
theorem Opn.erdos_257_lacunary_union :
    ∀ (r : ℕ) (c : Fin r → ℕ → ℕ), 0 < r → (∀ i, 0 < c i 0) →
    (∀ i j, (2 * r + 1) ^ (r + 1) * c i j ≤ c i (j + 1)) →
    Irrational (∑' n : (⋃ i, Set.range (c i) : Set ℕ), (1 : ℝ) / (2 ^ n.1 - 1)) := by
  sorry

Selected statement pairwise coprime A with Σ 1/a < ∞ (Erdős 1968)

variant-c91009b9Rename or correct →

status
proved
attempts
0 attempts

Restated as hole h_coprime of erdos-257 (split by 20261023T133841Z-t1023-f1-partial.lean).

What is claimed

$\displaystyle \forall\, A \subseteq \mathbb{N} \text{ infinite, pairwise coprime, with } \sum_{n\in A}\frac1n < \infty:\qquad \sum_{n\in A}\frac{1}{2^n-1} \ \text{ is irrational}.$

"Pairwise coprime" is Mathlib's Set.Pairwise Nat.Coprime: $\gcd(m,n) = 1$ for distinct $m, n \in A$. The hypothesis $\sum_{n\in A} \frac1n < \infty$ is Lean's Summable of $n \mapsto \frac1n$ on $A$ (with $\frac10 = 0$). Sums are Lean's tsum over the set, the ordinary sum of a convergent nonnegative series; the term at $n = 0$ is Lean's $\frac{1}{2^0-1} = \frac10 = 0$.

(Written by t1022-e; filed by t1022-k.)

unverified · history

Lean
theorem Opn.erdos_257_coprime_summable :
    ∀ A : Set ℕ, A.Infinite → A.Pairwise Nat.Coprime →
      Summable (fun n : A => (1 : ℝ) / n.1) →
      Irrational (∑' n : A, (1 : ℝ) / (2 ^ n.1 - 1)) := by
  sorry

Selected statement the case A = sums of two squares

variant-d08712d9Rename or correct →

status
proved
attempts
0 attempts

Restated as hole h_two_squares of erdos-257 (split by 20261023T133841Z-t1023-f1-partial.lean).

What is claimed

$\displaystyle \sum_{m \,\in\, \{a^2+b^2 \,:\, a,\, b \in \mathbb{N}\}} \frac{1}{2^m-1} \ \text{ is irrational}.$

The sum runs over the set of sums of two squares, $\{0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 8, 9, 10, 13, \dots\}$, so each such $m$ is counted once however many representations $m = a^2 + b^2$ it has. Here $\mathbb{N} = \{0, 1, 2, \dots\}$, so every square, $0$ included, is a sum of two squares. The sum is Lean's tsum over the set, the ordinary sum of a convergent nonnegative series; the term at $m = 0$ is Lean's $\frac{1}{2^0-1} = \frac10 = 0$.

unverified · history

  sorry

Selected statement eventually lacunary of ratio two ⇒ irrational

variant-d144ccc1Rename or correct →

status
proved
attempts
0 attempts

Restated as hole h_ratio_two of erdos-257 (split by 20261023T133841Z-t1023-f1-partial.lean).

What is claimed

$\displaystyle \forall A \subseteq \mathbb{N}:\quad A \text{ infinite},\ 0 \notin A,\ \bigl(\exists N\ \forall m, n \in A:\ N \le n < m \Rightarrow 2n \le m\bigr) \ \Longrightarrow\ \sum_{n \in A} \frac{1}{2^n - 1} \notin \mathbb{Q}.$

The sum is over the elements of $A$ (Lean's tsum over the subtype); it converges because $\frac{1}{2^n-1} \le 2^{1-n}$ for $n \ge 1$. The hypothesis $0 \notin A$ excludes the one index where Lean's division by $2^0 - 1 = 0$ would give the junk value $0$. "Irrational" is Mathlib's Irrational: the real number is not the image of any rational.

The docstring's examples are to be read with $0$ left out: $\{2^k - 1\}$ means $\{2^k - 1 : k \ge 1\}$, since $k = 0$ gives $0$, which the hypothesis $0 \notin A$ excludes; likewise a finite set joined to an example must not contain $0$.

(The last paragraph is t1022-k's, from t1022-n's review; the rest is t1022-b's.)

unverified · history

Lean
theorem Opn.erdos_257_eventually_ratio_two :
    ∀ (A : Set ℕ), A.Infinite → 0 ∉ A →
    (∃ N : ℕ, ∀ m ∈ A, ∀ n ∈ A, N ≤ n → n < m → 2 * n ≤ m) →
    Irrational (∑' n : A, (1 : ℝ) / (2 ^ n.1 - 1)) := by
  sorry

How it is being proved

Proved by Proof.lean (merged in 2df0ed117ad5) · attestation 000891.
